add_namespace = yugoslavia
add_namespace = yug_tripartite_pact

country_event = {  # Vidovdan
	id = yugoslavia.100
	title = yugoslavia.100.t
	desc = yugoslavia.100.d
	picture = GFX_event_vidovdan
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = YUG
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { days = 365 id = yugoslavia.101 }
			country_event = { days = 730 id = yugoslavia.101 }
			country_event = { days = 1095 id = yugoslavia.101 }
			country_event = { days = 1460 id = yugoslavia.101 }
			country_event = { days = 1825 id = yugoslavia.101 }
			country_event = { days = 2190 id = yugoslavia.101 }
			country_event = { days = 2555 id = yugoslavia.101 }
			country_event = { days = 2920 id = yugoslavia.101 }
			country_event = { days = 3285 id = yugoslavia.101 }
			country_event = { days = 3650 id = yugoslavia.101 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.100.a
		add_political_power = 10
	}
}

country_event = {  # Anton Korošec Submits!
	id = yugoslavia.102
	title = yugoslavia.102.t
	desc = yugoslavia.102.d
	picture = GFX_korosec_submits
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = YUG
		has_war = no
		is_subject = no
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Alexander I"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
		controls_state = 102
		controls_state = 838
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.102.a
		add_political_power = 40
		set_country_flag = YUG_korosec_cooperates
		clr_country_flag = YUG_korosec_exiled
		set_variable = {
			var = slovene_opinion
			value = 3
		}
	}
}

country_event = {  # New Prime Minister
	id = yugoslavia.105
	title = yugoslavia.105.t
	desc = yugoslavia.105.d
	picture = GFX_skupstina
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = YUG
		has_war = no
		is_subject = no
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Alexander I"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.105.a # Uzunovic
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_game_rule = { rule = YUG_paths_rule option = YUG_PATHS_ROYAL }
			}
		}
		add_political_power = 80
		add_popularity = { ideology = democratic popularity = 0.03 }
		add_popularity = { ideology = monarchism popularity = 0.01 }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = YUG_hog_Milan_Srskic
			add_idea = YUG_hog_Nikola_Uzunovic
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			complete_national_focus = YUG_2nd_uzunovic
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.105.b # Zivkovic
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				is_historical_focus_on = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_game_rule = { rule = YUG_paths_rule option = YUG_PATHS_JRZ }
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_game_rule = { rule = YUG_paths_rule option = YUG_PATHS_OPPOSITION }
			}
		}
		add_political_power = 80
		add_popularity = { ideology = neutrality popularity = 0.03 }
		add_popularity = { ideology = monarchism popularity = 0.01 }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = YUG_hog_Milan_Srskic
			add_idea = YUG_hog_Petar_Zivkovic
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			complete_national_focus = YUG_2nd_zivkovic
		}
	}
}

country_event = {  # King Alexander Is Murdered!
	id = yugoslavia.114
	title = yugoslavia.114.t
	desc = {
		text = yugoslavia.114.d
		trigger = {
			date < 1941.09.06
			NOT = { has_country_flag = YUG_jns_won }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = YUG_opposition_won }
		}
	}
	desc = { #(JRZ or UO)
		text = yugoslavia.120.d
		trigger = {
			date < 1941.09.06
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = YUG_jns_won
				has_country_flag = YUG_opposition_won
			}
		}
	}
	desc = { # Peter is old enough
		text = yugoslavia.244.d
		trigger = {
			date > 1941.09.06
		}
	}
	picture = GFX_alexander_killed
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = YUG
		# NOT = { has_country_flag = YUG_jns_won }
		# NOT = { has_country_flag = YUG_opposition_won }
	}
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			# kill_country_leader = yes
			retire_character = YUG_king_alexander_I
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: King Alexander is murdered"
			set_country_flag = YUG_alexander_is_dead
			add_named_threat = {
				threat = 1
				name = yugoslavia.114.t
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					date < 1941.09.06
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = YUG_jns_won
						has_country_flag = YUG_opposition_won
					}
				}
				add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = -0.05 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = monarchism popularity = -0.25 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = nationalism popularity = -0.10 }
				add_stability = -0.25
				set_country_flag = YUG_regency
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = YUG_jns_won
						has_country_flag = YUG_opposition_won
					}
				}
				add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = -0.05 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = monarchism popularity = -0.15 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = nationalism popularity = -0.10 }
				add_stability = -0.15
				set_country_flag = YUG_regency
			}
			else_if = { #Peter is of age
				limit = { date > 1941.09.06 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = -0.05 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = monarchism popularity = -0.15 }
				add_popularity = { ideology = nationalism popularity = -0.10 }
				add_stability = -0.25
			}
			country_event = {
				id = yugoslavia.115
				days = 3
			}

			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_marseille }
				news_event = {
					id = yugoslavia.2000
				}
				clr_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_marseille
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_zagreb }
				news_event = {
					id = yugoslavia.2001
				}
				clr_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_zagreb
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_belgrade }
				news_event = {
					id = yugoslavia.2002
				}
				clr_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_belgrade
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_cetinje }
				news_event = {
					id = yugoslavia.2003
				}
				clr_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_cetinje
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_bled }
				news_event = {
					id = yugoslavia.2005
				}
				clr_country_flag = YUG_alexander_dead_in_bled
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.114.a # Prince Paul
		trigger = {
			date < 1941.09.06
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 70
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_game_rule = { rule = YUG_paths_rule option = YUG_PATHS_ROYAL }
			}
		}
		add_popularity = { ideology = monarchism popularity = 0.05 }
		custom_effect_tooltip = newline_tt

		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_prince_paul
			country_leader={
				ideology=military
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_prince_paul
			country_leader={
				ideology=semi_constitutional
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_prince_paul
			country_leader={
				ideology=nationalist
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_prince_paul
			country_leader={
				ideology=conservatism
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_prince_paul
			country_leader={
				ideology=agrarianism
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_prince_paul
			country_leader={
				ideology=market_liberal
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_prince_paul
			country_leader={
				ideology=liberalism
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_prince_paul
			country_leader={
				ideology=dem_socialism
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_prince_paul
			country_leader={
				ideology=clerical
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}

		hidden_effect = {
			complete_national_focus = YUG_prince_paul_regency
		}
	}

		name = yugoslavia.114.b # JNS
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = YUG_jns_won

				var = jmo_seats
				value = 7
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = jrss_seats
				value = 75
			}

				var = sls_seats
				value = 0
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = government_seats
				value = 82
			}
		}
		#below 20
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_stability < 0.2
				NOT = {has_country_flag = YUG_spaho_cooperates}
				NOT = {has_country_flag = YUG_korosec_cooperates}

			}

country_event = { # The Results Are In!
	id = yugoslavia.137
	title = yugoslavia.137.t
	desc = yugoslavia.137.d
	picture = GFX_yugoslavia_coa
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = YUG
		is_subject = no
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.137.a
		hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { srb_treaty < 6 }
					check_variable = { cro_treaty < 5 }
				}
			}
			country_event = {
			id = yugoslavia.138
			days = 1
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					check_variable = { srb_treaty < 6 }
					check_variable = { cro_treaty < 5 }
				}
			}
			country_event = {
			id = yugoslavia.139
			days = 1
			}
		}
		}
	}
}

			id = yugoslavia.2004
			days = 1
		}

		}

			set_variable = { montenegrin_opinion = 3 }

			limit = {
				check_variable = { srb_treaty > 9 }
			}

			set_variable = { montenegrin_opinion = 4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { cro_treaty < 8 }
			}
			set_variable = { croat_opinion = 2 }
		}
		if = {

		}
		}

		}
		text = yugoslavia.168.pm.d
	}
	picture = GFX_pribicevic
	is_triggered_only = yes

	}

		add_popularity = { ideology = social_liberalism popularity = -0.03 }
	}

country_event = {  #Second meeting with Prince Paul - Failure
	id = yugoslavia.175
	title = yugoslavia.175.t
	desc = yugoslavia.175.d
	picture = GFX_army_in_zagreb
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = YUG
		is_subject = no
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Prince Paul"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = {has_completed_focus = YUG_influence_his_advisors}
			NOT = {has_completed_focus = YUG_find_common_ground}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.175.a #ok then
		ai_chance = { base = 40 }
		add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberalism
				popularity = -0.1
			}
		add_popularity = {
			ideology = monarchism
			popularity = -0.02
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = croat_opinion
			value = 2
		}
		add_stability = 0.03
		add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = 0.08 }
		add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.08 }
	}
	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.175.b #go for escalation
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
		add_popularity = {
				ideology = social_liberalism
				popularity = -0.04
			}
			add_popularity = {
				ideology = monarchism
				popularity = -0.02
			}
		set_variable = {
			var = croat_opinion
			value = 1
		}
		add_stability = -0.15
		add_popularity = { ideology = fascism popularity = 0.04 }
		add_popularity = { ideology = communism popularity = 0.04 }
		#tbd when there are coups
	}
}

		set_country_flag = YUG_hss_government
		set_country_flag = YUG_sds_government

	id = yugoslavia.208
	title = yugoslavia.208.t
	desc = yugoslavia.208.d
	picture = GFX_yugoslavia_joins_pact
	fire_only_once = yes
	timeout_days = 5
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 3
	}
	trigger = {
		tag = YUG
		is_subject = no
		has_war = no
		date > 1941.1.1
		GER = {
			OR = {
				has_war_with = ENG
				has_war_with = FRA
				has_war_with = SOV
			}
		}
		OR = {
			is_neighbor_of = GER
			any_neighbor_country = {
				OR = {
					is_in_faction_with = GER
					is_subject_of = GER
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FRA = { has_war_with = GER }
			}
			FRA = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				BUL = {
					is_neighbor_of = YUG
					is_in_faction_with = GER
				}
			}
			AND = {
				HUN = {
					is_neighbor_of = YUG
					is_in_faction_with = GER
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.208.a #Join
		ai_chance = {
			base = 50
		}
		add_political_power = -50
		hidden_effect = {
			GER = {
				country_event = {
					id = yugoslavia.1028
					days = 3
				}
			}
		}
	}
	# option = {

	# 	ai_chance = {

country_event = { #King Peter becomes old enough
	id = yugoslavia.245
	title = yugoslavia.245.t
	desc = yugoslavia.245.d
	picture = GFX_king_peter
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		date > 1941.09.06
		is_subject = no
		has_country_flag = YUG_regency
		NOT = {
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Alexander I"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Peter II"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
	}
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			# kill_country_leader = yes
			add_stability = -0.15
			add_popularity = { ideology = monarchism popularity = 0.05 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.245.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = newline_tt
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_king_peter_II
			country_leader={
				ideology=military
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_king_peter_II
			country_leader={
				ideology=semi_constitutional
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_king_peter_II
			country_leader={
				ideology=nationalist
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_king_peter_II
			country_leader={
				ideology=conservatism
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_king_peter_II
			country_leader={
				ideology=agrarianism
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_king_peter_II
			country_leader={
				ideology=market_liberal
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_king_peter_II
			country_leader={
				ideology=liberalism
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_king_peter_II
			country_leader={
				ideology=dem_socialism
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
	}
}

				var = ygw_mnt_g_pow
				value = 20

			set_country_flag = ygw_mac_ben_m

				value = 20

					}

			}

country_event = {  # Klubasi and Zborasi check
	id = yugoslavia.262
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			has_idea = YUG_struggle_klubasi_winning
			has_idea = YUG_struggle_balanced
			has_idea = YUG_struggle_zborasi_winning
		}
	}
	option = {
		country_event = {
			id = yugoslavia.262
			days = 50
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { klubasi_power > 180 }
			}
			YUG = {
				add_ideas = YUG_struggle_klubasi_won
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_klubasi_winning
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_balanced
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_zborasi_winning
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { klubasi_power > 120 }
				check_variable = { klubasi_power < 180 }
				NOT = {	has_idea = YUG_struggle_klubasi_winning	}
			}
			YUG = {
				add_ideas = YUG_struggle_klubasi_winning
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_balanced
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_zborasi_winning
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { klubasi_power < 120 }
				check_variable = { zborasi_power < 120 }
				NOT = {	has_idea = YUG_struggle_balanced	}
			}
			YUG = {
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_klubasi_winning
				add_ideas = YUG_struggle_balanced
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_zborasi_winning
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { zborasi_power > 120 }
				check_variable = { zborasi_power < 180 }
				NOT = {	has_idea = YUG_struggle_zborasi_winning	}
			}
			YUG = {
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_klubasi_winning
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_balanced
				add_ideas = YUG_struggle_zborasi_winning
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { zborasi_power > 180 }
			}
			YUG = {
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_klubasi_winning
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_balanced
				remove_ideas = YUG_struggle_zborasi_winning
				add_ideas = YUG_struggle_zborasi_won
			}
		}
	}
}

country_event = {  #Talks with Romania
	id = yugoslavia.1000
	title = yugoslavia.1000.t
	desc = yugoslavia.1000.d
	picture = GFX_bogoljub_jevtic
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = ROM
		has_war = no
		is_subject = no
		YUG = {
		has_country_leader = {
			name = "Alexander I"
			ruling_only = yes
		}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.1000.a #agree
		ai_chance = { base = 95 }

		ROM = {
			 	add_opinion_modifier = { target = YUG modifier = medium_increase }
		}
		YUG = {
			 	add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROM modifier = medium_increase }
		}
		YUG = { country_event = { days = 5 id = yugoslavia.106 }}
	}
	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.1000.b #refuse
		ai_chance = { base = 5 }

		ROM = {
			 	add_opinion_modifier = { target = YUG modifier = medium_decrease }
		}
		YUG = {
			 	add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROM modifier = medium_decrease }
		}
		YUG = { country_event = { days = 5 id = yugoslavia.107 }}
	}
}

### EVENTS DOUBLED FOR HIS MAJESTY KING ALEXANDER ###

country_event = {  #PM Pribicevic
	id = yugoslavia.9160
	title = yugoslavia.160.t
	desc = yugoslavia.160.d
	picture = GFX_pribicevic
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = {
		tag = YUG
		is_subject = no
		has_stability > 0.4
		NOT = {
			has_country_leader = {
				name = "Petar Živković"
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = yugoslavia.160.a
		load_focus_tree = { tree = YUG_early_opposition keep_completed = yes }
		add_political_power = 150
		add_popularity = { ideology = social_liberalism popularity = 0.3 }
		remove_ideas_with_trait = hog
		add_ideas = YUG_hog_Svetozar_Pribicevic
		remove_ideas_with_trait = frnmin
		add_ideas = YUG_frn_Ivan_Subasic
		remove_ideas_with_trait = ecomin
		add_ideas = YUG_eco_Vladko_Macek
		remove_ideas_with_trait = secmin
		add_ideas = YUG_sec_Srdjan_Budisavljevic
		set_variable = {
				var = croat_opinion
				value = 4
		}
		set_variable = {
				var = serb_opinion
				value = 4
		}
		set_variable = {
				var = montenegrin_opinion
				value = 4
		}
		set_politics = {
				ruling_party = social_liberalism
		}
		COALITION_RESET = yes
		set_country_flag = YUG_hss_government

country_event = {
	id = yug_tripartite_pact.2
	title = yug_tripartite_pact.2.t
	desc = yug_tripartite_pact.2.d
	picture = GFX_news_event_YUG_demonstrations_1941_coup
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes

	option = {
		name = yug_tripartite_pact.2.a

		retire_country_leader = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_government = neutrality }
			}
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = neutrality
			}
		}
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = neutrality
			long_name = "Government of National Unity"
			name = "GNU"
		}
		COALITION_RESET = yes
		COALITION_add_cen = yes #ZS
		COALITION_add_con = yes #NRS
		COALITION_add_marlib = yes #DS
		COALITION_add_soclib = yes #HSS

		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = YUG_king_peter_II
			country_leader={
				ideology=military
				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}
			promote_leader = yes
		}
		add_country_leader_role = {

			country_leader={
				ideology=semi_constitutional
				expire="1965.1.1.1"

				expire="1965.1.1.1"
				id=-1
			}

		# 	promote_character = { ideology = semi_constitutional }
		# 	promote_character = { ideology = liberalism }

			GER = {

					id = yug_tripartite_pact.3
					hours = 2
				}
			}

	id = yug_tripartite_pact.3

	desc = yug_tripartite_pact.3.d

	fire_only_once = yes

			decision = GER_fuhrer_directive_n25

		activate_decision = GER_fuhrer_directive_n25
	}
